Motivation: 扩散模型依赖随机输入，但在有限精度硬件上，"随机性"实际上是由确定性数值轨道（伪随机规则）实现的。这些可访问的轨道结构可能成为可学习的输入，影响训练和生成过程。然而，此前研究未系统探索伪随机源的选择如何作为模型依赖的结构化输入影响扩散模型性能。

Method: 采用两种测量手段：(1) 小型多层感知机（MLP）从近期历史预测轨道下一个值，衡量一般序列可预测性；(2) 扩散探针，用在线随机张量替换真实图像但保留扩散架构和训练目标，衡量目标系统能否利用轨道结构。在控制边缘统计量并排除动力学与有限精度失败后，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上进行实验。

Result: 不同伪随机轨道产生显著不同的扩散损失和生成质量。两种测量与宏观生成退化均显示强秩相关，但局部排序不同。经IID基线归一化后，探针损失与真实数据扩散损失近似遵循经验幂律，且在两个数据集上表现出不同指数。

Conclusion: 伪随机源不仅是分布选择，更是模型依赖的结构化输入。扩散模型对哈希种子/伪随机规则的敏感性表明，在选择训练和推理的随机源时，需要考虑其与模型架构及数据集的交互作用。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely on stochastic inputs, yet on finite-precision hardware, the "randomness" they consume is realized as deterministic numerical orbits generated by pseudorandom rules. Accessible orbit structure can become a learnable input and affect both training and generation because the realized loss and its gradient depend on the concrete pseudorandom values consumed at each optimization step. A small multilayer perceptron predicts the next value of an orbit from its recent history, measuring general sequence predictability. A diffusion probe replaces real images with online random tensors while preserving the diffusion architecture and training objective, measuring whether the target system can exploit orbit structure. After controlling marginal statistics and screening out clear dynamical and finite-precision failures, the remaining orbits still produce markedly different diffusion losses and generation quality on MNIST and CIFAR-10. Both measures show strong rank correlations with macroscopic generation degradation, although their local rankings differ. After normalization by the IID baseline, the probe loss and the real-data diffusion loss approximately follow an empirical power law, with different exponents on the two datasets. These results suggest that a pseudorandom source is not only a distributional choice, but also a model-dependent structured input.

</details>


### [163] [Smooth Reparameterizations of Functions on Simplicial Product Spaces: Applications to Probabilistic Tensor Decomposition and Functional Data Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02576)
*Shashwat Kumar,Arafat Rahman,Anuj Srivastava,P. -A. Absil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了定义在单纯形乘积空间上的优化问题，提出用光滑的逐元素严格凸重参数化替代乘积单纯形，将约束优化转化为流形上的无约束优化。证明了重参数化后流形上的二阶KKT点对应原问题在乘积单纯形上的弱二阶KKT点，并据此提出Riemannian梯度下降（RGD）算法，在张量分解和函数数据配准中优于投影梯度下降（PGD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重要的优化问题（如低秩离散多元概率分布学习中的单纯形约束张量分解、SRVF表示下的函数数据配准）定义在乘积单纯形上，该空间的非光滑边界和约束性质给优化算法带来挑战，需要寻找更好的替代优化策略。

Method: （1）提出将乘积单纯形替换为光滑、逐元素严格凸的重参数化，使原约束优化问题转化为流形上的无约束优化；（2）从理论上证明重参数化后流形上的二阶KKT点映射回原空间对应弱二阶KKT点，保证了最优性保持；（3）基于该重参数化设计Riemannian梯度下降（RGD）算法进行求解。

Result: 实验表明提出的RGD算法在求解重参数化问题时优于投影梯度下降（PGD）算法，并且在函数曲线配准中能够更忠实地保留原始函数的形状特征。

Conclusion: 通过光滑严格凸重参数化可以将乘积单纯形上的约束优化转化为流形上的无约束优化，且在保证最优性（弱二阶KKT条件）的前提下，RGD算法较PGD在收敛性和结果保真度方面均具有优势。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems defined on product spaces of simplices. Examples of this class of problems include learning low-rank discrete multivariate probability distributions via simplex constrained tensor decomposition and performing functional data registration under the Square Root Velocity Function (SRVF) representation. In this work, we demonstrate the feasibility of replacing the product simplex with a smooth, elementwise strictly convex reparameterization, resulting in an unconstrained optimization problem on a manifold. We show that performing such a reparameterization results in the second order Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points on the smooth manifold being mapped to the weak second order KKT points on the product simplex. This leads to a Riemannian Gradient Descent (RGD) algorithm for solving the reparameterized problem, which outperforms Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), and provides a more faithful representation of the original function shapes while performing curve registration.

</details>


### [164] [GradCuit: Credit-Assigned Gradient Flow Enables Robust and Interpretable Test-Time Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02585)
*Zhaoxin Yu,Qi Shen,Hengli Li,Zhaowei Zhang,Song-Chun Zhu,Chi Zhang,Zilong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GradCuit是一种通过直接在Transformer层的潜在表示中插入可优化连续状态来进行测试时推理优化的方法，使续生成token的对数概率通过剩余Transformer块直接获得指向所有前置潜在状态的梯度路径，实现从整个续生成到潜在状态的全序列级奖励加权梯度分配。在5个指令微调骨干模型、3个推理基准和2种答案格式上平均准确率达64.5%，超越思维链提示6.6个百分点、超越最强竞争方法2.4个百分点，并通过直接优化内部推理开辟了鲁棒且可解释的测试时扩展新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于优化的潜在推理方法通过解码token连接潜在状态与推理轨迹，导致序列级信用分配不直接，难以理解潜在更新如何塑造后续推理。作者旨在解决这一间接性缺陷，使潜在状态的优化能够直接接收来自整个续生成的奖励加权梯度信号。

Method: GradCuit在选定的Transformer层中将可优化潜在状态插入提示的隐藏表示与生成续编之间。利用因果自注意力的特性，每个续生成token的对数概率通过剩余Transformer块对所有前置潜在状态存在可微路径，从而将整个续生成的奖励加权梯度直接分配到潜在状态。该方法在测试时保持模型参数冻结，优化实例特定的连续状态。

Result: 在5个指令微调骨干模型、3个推理基准和2种答案格式上，GradCuit平均准确率达64.5%，优于思维链提示6.6个百分点、优于最强竞争方法2.4个百分点。在7种学习率设置下，其准确率标准差从1.53降至0.82，且随机游走变体仍与LatentSeek相当。可解释性分析显示潜在影响集中于推理连接词token，且早期到中期的Transformer层是最有效的优化空间。

Conclusion: GradCuit通过直接优化内部推理而非仅仅重新生成、采样或重排序输出来实现鲁棒且可解释的测试时扩展，为LLM测试时推理优化开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Optimization-based latent reasoning improves large language model outputs by optimizing instance-specific continuous states at test time while keeping model parameters frozen. Existing methods, however, typically connect these states to the reasoning trajectory through decoded tokens, making sequence-level credit assignment indirect and obscuring how latent updates shape subsequent reasoning. We introduce GradCuit (gradient through circuit), which inserts optimizable latent states at a selected Transformer layer between the hidden representations of the prompt and the generated continuation. Causal self-attention provides every continuation-token log-probability with a differentiable path to every preceding latent state through the remaining Transformer blocks, enabling reward-weighted gradients from the entire continuation to be assigned directly to the latents. Across five instruction-tuned backbones, three reasoning benchmarks, and two answer formats, GradCuit achieves an average accuracy of 64.5%, outperforming chain-of-thought prompting by 6.6 percentage points and the strongest competing method by 2.4 points. GradCuit also demonstrates greater robustness: across seven learning-rate settings, it consistently outperforms LatentSeek while reducing the standard deviation of accuracy from 1.53 to 0.82, and even its random-walk variant remains competitive with LatentSeek. For interpretability, token-level gradient attribution reveals that latent influence concentrates on reasoning-connector tokens, while layer analysis identifies early-to-middle Transformer layers as the most effective optimization space. By directly optimizing internal reasoning from outcome feedback, GradCuit opens a new axis of robust and interpretable test-time scaling, where LLMs adapt how they reason rather than merely regenerate, sample, or rerank outputs.

</details>


### [165] [onepot-Bench 0: towards lab-aware in silico chemistry benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02595)
*Brandon Wang,Andrei S. Tyrin,Daniil A. Boiko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了onepot-Bench 0，一个用于评估语言模型在湿实验室合成化学能力的专有基准套件，包含三个互补评估：ChemAbacus（工具无关的化学信息学素养和数值推理）、SynthRefusal（安全与拒答行为）和SynthBench（反应结果预测和催化剂选择，使用私有实验数据）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在实验室科学中扮演越来越重要的角色，但现有评估难以精确衡量其在物理实验室中做出可靠决策所需的能力。现有评估常依赖可能已出现在模型训练语料中的公开数据，且很少测量湿实验室执行所需的能力。

Method: 构建专有基准套件onepot-Bench 0，包含三个互补的评估模块：ChemAbacus测量工具无关的化学信息学素养和数值推理能力；SynthRefusal评估模型在良性的、受控的和设计药物目标上的安全与拒答行为；SynthBench使用实验室内部生成的私有实验数据评估反应结果预测和催化剂选择能力。

Result: onepot-Bench 0共同从基础能力、可靠性和深层知识三个维度探测模型在实验室中可靠表现所需的技能，且所有数据均为专有或私有生成，避免与训练语料重叠的污染问题。

Conclusion: onepot-Bench 0提供了一个更全面、更贴近真实湿实验室场景的语言模型化学能力评估框架，弥补了现有评估在数据隐私性和实际实验能力覆盖上的不足。

Abstract: Language models are playing an increasingly important role in laboratory science, performing tasks such as experiment planning, execution, and post-hoc analysis. However, precisely measuring their abilities is difficult, as scientific capabilities require a mixture of both problem-solving skills and domain-specific intuition. Existing evaluations rarely measure the capabilities required to make reliable decisions in a physical laboratory and often rely on public data that may have appeared in model training corpora.
  We introduce onepot-Bench 0, a proprietary benchmark suite for evaluating language models on synthetic chemistry capabilities relevant to wet-lab execution. onepot-Bench 0 comprises three complementary evaluations: ChemAbacus measures tool-free cheminformatics literacy and numerical reasoning; SynthRefusal characterizes safety and refusal behavior across a variety of benign, controlled, and designer-drug targets; and SynthBench evaluates reaction-outcome prediction and catalyst selection using private experimental data generated in our laboratory. Together, these evaluations probe basic competency, reliability, and deeper knowledge, all skills which are required for reliable performance in the lab.

</details>
